```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
```

```
        }
```

Please generate JML specifications for the Java program given below.
```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

- Every loop must have loop invariants ("maintaining" clauses).
- Do NOT use assumptions ("assume" clause).
- Do NOT specify exceptional behavior ("signals" clauses). Exclude all exception-causing states via preconditions ("requires" clause).
- If the method returns a newly allocated object, state the freshness with "ensures \fresh(\result);".
- Provide a STRONG and COMPLETE specification: cover all functional behavior, state effects, and return value. The specification must contain enough detail to allow to reconstruct the original code unambiguously.

```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case: null or empty array -> true
      @ requires digits == null || digits.length == 0;
      @ assigns \nothing;
      @ ensures \result == true;
      @ also
      @ // Case: non-null, non-empty array -> check strobogrammatic property
      @ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
      @ assigns \nothing;
      @ ensures \result == (
      @    (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
      @         ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
      @    )
      @    &&
      @    // If odd length, middle digit must be 0,1,or 8
      @    ( (digits.length % 2 == 1) 
      @        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
      @        : true
      @    )
      @ );
      @ behaviors disjoint;
      @ behaviors complete;
      @*/
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
          @ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i ==>
          @     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases (len/2) - i;
          @*/
```
```
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {

                return false;
```

```
    //@        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@        : true
    //@    )
    //@ );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i ==> 
```
```
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
```
```
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
```
```
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    /*@ 
      @ // Case: null or empty array -> true
      @ requires digits == null || digits.length == 0;
      @ assigns \nothing;
      @ ensures \result == true;
      @ also
      @ // Case: non-null, non-empty array -> check strobogrammatic property
      @ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
      @ assigns \nothing;
      @ ensures \result == (
      @    (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
      @         ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
      @          (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
      @    )
      @    &&
      @    // If odd length, middle digit must be 0,1,or 8
      @    ( (digits.length % 2 == 1) 
      @        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
      @        : true
      @    )
      @ );
      @ behaviors disjoint;
      @ behaviors complete;
      @*/
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        /*@ 
          @ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
          @ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i ==>
          @     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
          @      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
          @ loop_writes i;
          @ decreases (len/2) - i;
          @*/
```
```
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@   (
    //@     (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k < digits.length/2 &&
    //@         !((digits[k] == 0 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 0) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 1 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 1) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 8 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 8) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 6 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 9) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 9 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 6))
    //@         && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < k ==>
    //@               ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@           )
    //@     )
    //@     ||
    //@     ( digits.length % 2 == 1
    //@       && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@             ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@         )
    //@       && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@     )
    //@   );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

assistant: ```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    /*
     * Strong, complete specification matching the implementation exactly.
     */

    //@ // Case 1: null or empty array -> method returns true immediately
    //@ requires digits == null || digits.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@ // Case 2: non-null, non-empty array and every checked mirrored pair (and middle if any)
    //@ // satisfies the allowed strobogrammatic mappings -> method returns true
    //@ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@            ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@             (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@             (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@             (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@             (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6)))
    //@      && (digits.length % 2 == 0
    //@          || (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8))
    //@      ==> \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@ // Case 3: non-null, non-empty array and method returns false exactly when
    //@ // there exists a first index k (0 <= k < len/2) where the mirrored pair is invalid,
    //@ // with all earlier pairs valid, OR all mirrored pairs are valid but the middle digit
    //@ // (when len is odd) is invalid. This encodes the early return in the loop and the
    //@ // subsequent middle-digit test.
    //@ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == false ==>
    //@   (
    //@     // First kind of failure: an earliest mismatched pair
    //@     (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k < digits.length/2 &&
    //@         !((digits[k] == 0 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 0) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 1 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 1) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 8 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 8) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 6 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 9) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 9 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 6))
    //@         && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < k;
    //@                ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@                 (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@                 (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@                 (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@                 (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6)))
    //@     )
    //@     ||
    //@     // Second kind of failure: all pairs valid but middle digit invalid (odd length)
    //@     ( digits.length % 2 == 1
    //@       && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@             ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6)))
    //@       && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@     )
    //@   );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        /* Loop: iterate i from 0 to len/2 - 1, return false on first invalid pair.
         * Loop specifications mirror the behavior of the implementation.
         */
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@ // Case: non-null, non-empty array -> exactly the strobogrammatic check performed by the code
    //@ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ // The method returns true iff every mirrored pair (j, len-j-1) satisfies one of the allowed mappings
    //@ // and, when length is odd, the middle digit is one of {0,1,8}.
    //@ ensures \result == (
    //@    (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@         ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@    )
    //@    &&
    //@    ((digits.length % 2 == 1)
    //@        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@        : true
    //@    )
    //@ );
    //@ // Make explicit the dual reason for returning false: either some mirrored pair is invalid
    //@ // or (for odd length) the middle digit is invalid.
    //@ ensures !\result ==>
    //@    ((\exists int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@         !((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@    )
    //@    ||
    //@    (digits.length % 2 == 1 && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8))
    //@    );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@          (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@    )
    //@    &&
    //@    ((digits.length % 2 == 1)
    //@        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@        : true
    //@    )
    //@ );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

- Return Value: Define exactly what is returned (not just characteristics like positive/negative).
- Bounds and Ranges: Specify strict limits on indices, sizes, and values.

- Minimality/Maximality: Specify conditions related to the search (e.g., no earlier index satisfies the predicate) or "not found" conditions.
- Ordering and Permutation: If the code sorts or reorders, specify preservation of elements (permutation) and the sorting order.
- Allocation/Aliasing: Use "\fresh(\result)" and specify non-aliasing relationships when the code returns new allocations.

assistant: ```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    //@ // Case: null or empty array -> true
    //@ requires digits == null || digits.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@ // Case: non-null, non-empty array -> exactly the strobogrammatic check performed by the code
    //@ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ // The method returns true iff every mirrored pair (j, len-j-1) satisfies one of the allowed mappings
    //@ // and, when length is odd, the middle digit is one of {0,1,8}.
    //@ ensures \result == (
    //@    (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@         ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@    )
    //@    &&
    //@    ((digits.length % 2 == 1)
    //@        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@        : true
    //@    )
    //@ );
    //@ // Make explicit the dual reason for returning false: either some mirrored pair is invalid
    //@ // or (for odd length) the middle digit is invalid.
    //@ ensures !\result ==>
    //@    ((\exists int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@         !((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@           (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@    )
    //@    ||
    //@    (digits.length % 2 == 1 && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8))
    //@    );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    /*
     * Strong, complete specification matching the implementation exactly.
     */

    //@ requires digits == null || digits.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;
    //@ also
    //@ // Case 2: non-null, non-empty array and every checked mirrored pair (and middle if any)
    //@ // satisfies the allowed strobogrammatic mappings -> method returns true
    //@ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@            ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||

    //@ // there exists a first index k (0 <= k < len/2) where the mirrored pair is invalid,
    //@ // with all earlier pairs valid, OR all mirrored pairs are valid but the middle digit
    //@ // (when len is odd) is invalid. This encodes the early return in the loop and the
    //@ // subsequent middle-digit test.
    //@ requires digits != null && digits.length > 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == false ==>
    //@   (
    //@     // First kind of failure: an earliest mismatched pair
    //@     (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k < digits.length/2 &&
    //@         !((digits[k] == 0 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 0) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 1 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 1) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 8 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 8) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 6 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 9) ||

    //@                 (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@                 (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@                 (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6)))
    //@     )
    //@     ||
    //@     // Second kind of failure: all pairs valid but middle digit invalid (odd length)
    //@     ( digits.length % 2 == 1
    //@       && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@             ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6)))
    //@       && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@     )
    //@   );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {

        int len = digits.length;

        /* Loop: iterate i from 0 to len/2 - 1, return false on first invalid pair.
         * Loop specifications mirror the behavior of the implementation.
         */

        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));

        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;

            }
```

```
    //@          (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@          (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))

    //@        ? (digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@        : true
    //@    )
    //@ );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||

        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;

            int a = digits[i];

                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }

            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
```

```
public class IsStrobogrammatic {
    /*
     * Strong, complete specification matching the implementation exactly.
     *
     * Two disjoint behaviors:
     *  - digits == null || digits.length == 0  -> returns true
     *  - digits != null && digits.length > 0   -> result equals the exact predicate
     *    checked by the implementation. Additionally, when result is false, we
     *    characterize the cause: either there is an earliest mismatched mirrored
     *    pair (the loop's early return) or (for odd length) all pairs match but
     *    the middle digit is invalid.
     */

    //@ requires digits == null || digits.length == 0;
    //@ assigns \nothing;
    //@ ensures \result == true;

    //@ // Case 2: non-null, non-empty array -> result exactly matches the implementation's check
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;

        return true;
    }
```

```
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i ==> 
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
    //@ // (A) there is a first mirrored-pair index k < len/2 at which the pair is invalid,
    //@ //     and all earlier pairs are valid (this models the early return inside the loop),
    //@ // OR
    //@ // (B) all mirrored pairs are valid, but the middle digit (when len is odd) is invalid.
    //@ ensures !\result ==>
    //@   (
    //@     (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k < digits.length/2 &&
    //@         !((digits[k] == 0 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 0) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 1 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 1) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 8 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 8) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 6 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 9) ||
    //@           (digits[k] == 9 && digits[digits.length - k - 1] == 6))
    //@         && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < k ==>
    //@               ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@                (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@           )
    //@     )
    //@     ||
    //@     ( digits.length % 2 == 1
    //@       && (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < digits.length/2;
    //@             ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6))
    //@         )
    //@       && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@     )
    //@   );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i;
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
     * Strong, complete specification matching the implementation exactly.
```
```
     *
     * Two disjoint behaviors:
     *  - digits == null || digits.length == 0  -> returns true
     *  - digits != null && digits.length > 0   -> result equals the exact predicate
     *    checked by the implementation. Additionally, when result is false, we
     *    characterize the cause: either there is an earliest mismatched mirrored
     *    pair (the loop's early return) or (for odd length) all pairs match but
     *    the middle digit is invalid.
```

```
    //@             ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 0) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 1 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 1) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 8 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 8) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 6 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 9) ||
    //@              (digits[j] == 9 && digits[digits.length - j - 1] == 6)))
    //@       && !(digits[digits.length/2] == 0 || digits[digits.length/2] == 1 || digits[digits.length/2] == 8)
    //@     )
    //@   );
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    public static boolean isStrobogrammatic(int[] digits) {
        if (digits == null || digits.length == 0) return true;

        int len = digits.length;

        /* Help the prover by asserting the facts established by the early return:
         * at this point we know we are in the non-null, non-empty case.
         */
        //@ assert digits != null && len == digits.length && len > 0;

        /* Loop contracts mirror the implementation: at step i all pairs j < i are valid. */
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i && i <= len/2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j && j < i ==>
        //@     ((digits[j] == 0 && digits[len - j - 1] == 0) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 1 && digits[len - j - 1] == 1) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 8 && digits[len - j - 1] == 8) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 6 && digits[len - j - 1] == 9) ||
        //@      (digits[j] == 9 && digits[len - j - 1] == 6));
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases (len/2) - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < len / 2; i++) {
            int a = digits[i];
            int b = digits[len - i - 1];

            if (!((a == 0 && b == 0) ||
                  (a == 1 && b == 1) ||
                  (a == 8 && b == 8) ||
                  (a == 6 && b == 9) ||
                  (a == 9 && b == 6))) {
                return false;
            }
        }

        if ((len % 2) == 1) {
            int mid = digits[len / 2];
            return mid == 0 || mid == 1 || mid == 8;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```
